# Open Nectar native iOS status

Updated: 2026-10-19, America/New_York. Branch: `ios-experimental`.

## Preservation baseline

- Repository: https://github.com/CeazyLoMein/Open-Nectar---Pikmin-Native-PC-Mobile-Port
- Starting local and fetched remote commit: `ed22a5ea9c362fba953b525d5358384426f23bf2`.
- Initial working tree already contained the three-edit `EnemyMgrBase.h` v1.4 iterator correction. Preserve and attribute it as pre-existing work.
- Pikmin 1 framebuffer correction is present in `games/pikmin1/pc_port/pc_window.cpp` (`pc_window_ios_drawable`, swap binding) and `gl/pc_gfx.cpp` (`pc_gfx_display_framebuffer`). Do not revert it.
- User reports Pikmin 1 title/menu/gameplay/Olimar movement/audio/touch working on iPhone 15 Pro Max, reportedly iOS 27.2. This session has no new hardware evidence.
- No game images, extracted assets, credentials, or signing material belong in Git.

## Verified build baseline

Build #9: https://github.com/CeazyLoMein/Open-Nectar---Pikmin-Native-PC-Mobile-Port/actions/runs/39167424765

- Commit: `ed22a5ea9c362fba953b525d5358384426f23bf2`.
- Pikmin 1 job `117793183742`: success, artifact `12150844781` (`nectar-pikmin1-experimental`).
- Pikmin 2 job `117793184024`: failure, artifact `12150864586` (`nectar-pikmin2-experimental`, compiler log only).
- Full workflow logs and Pikmin 2 compiler artifact retrieved directly from GitHub on October 19.
- Compiler artifact contains exactly one fatal compiler error: `EnemyMgrBase.h:95`, pointer-to-smaller-`int` conversion in `get(void*)`, compiling `plugProjectKandoU/vsTekiMgr.cpp`. No linker stage reached. Prior `vtxAnm.cpp` errors absent.

## Pikmin 1 investigation

- Downloaded Settings Menu Fix v1.4 ZIP exists but was not applied to the baseline.
- F1 settings mobile layout/render mapping/viewport are Android-only. iOS uses a fixed 640x480 desktop canvas.
- Title Options uses a separate fixed-canvas `settings/pc_glass_menu.cpp`; changing F1 gates alone does not address it.
- Settings GX/P2D text renders into the scene framebuffer before `pc_gfx_present`, then is linearly upscaled. Default scene render scale is 2/3.
- SDL UIKit defaults to scale 1 without `SDL_WINDOW_ALLOW_HIGHDPI`; touch icons draw after presentation through the independent overlay.
- Implemented candidate: retina drawable; retain logical-pixel scene workload during normal gameplay; full drawable resolution while F1 or title Advanced settings are open; 400-high mobile canvas and matching inverse touch mapping. Menu frame mode is latched for consistent presentation aspect; saved resolution/aspect settings remain unchanged. Conservative landscape margins are 56 horizontal and 24 bottom virtual units; dynamic UIKit safe-area support remains future work.
- Local verification: 115 C++17 x64 cases compiled/executed from actual changed geometry/sizing functions: four drawable shapes, inverse touch mapping, all five aspect modes, three render scales, both menus, explicit resolution override, close restoration, and mid-frame latch. MSVC emits only isolated harness shadow warnings. This does not compile the full iOS renderer.
- Changed source: pc_window.cpp high-DPI flag; gl/pc_gfx.cpp menu-frame resolution choice; settings/pc_settings.cpp mobile F1 gates/margins; settings/pc_glass_menu.cpp mobile Advanced layout, viewport/scissor, inverse tap/drag mapping. Existing SDL drawable framebuffer correction is retained.
- Hardware clarity/performance/touch validation remains required. Opening menus temporarily allocates a larger scene target and can cost memory/time. Original basic game Options/BLO typography is not redesigned by this patch.
- Existing right-thumb camera gesture is implemented in `touch/pc_touch.cpp`: separate movement and gesture fingers, button exclusion, one-finger rotation and two-finger zoom. Do not add a duplicate camera system before testing settings/behavior.

## Pikmin 2 verification

- Existing `EnemyMgrBase.h` change includes `<cstdint>`, encodes `getEnd()` through `std::intptr_t`, and decodes `get(void*)` through `std::intptr_t` before converting the bounded slot index to `int`.
- Local x64 MSVC C++14 `/W4 /WX` check compiles the actual changed inline methods extracted from the header. Execution passes empty/end handling and valid-slot pointer/index round trips. This is not an AppleClang/iOS build.
- Corrected the same pointer-as-index conversion in `src/plugProjectYamashitaU/enemyMgrBase.cpp::getNext`, including returned slot/end tokens. CMake includes this source through GAME_YAMASHITA_SOURCES. This is a verified source hazard adjacent to the logged header error, not a separately observed Build 9 compiler error.
- Extended x64 C++14 semantic check passes actual extracted getNext code with the -1 start sentinel, empty list, skipped inactive slots, live slots 2/6, and end-of-iteration handling.

## Pikmin 2 presentation investigation and candidate

- Its own `pc_port/gl/pc_gfx.cpp` still targeted FBO 0 in final present and touch overlay. Its own `pc_port/pc_window.cpp` swapped on iOS without binding UIKit's drawable framebuffer/color renderbuffer.
- Applied a P2-only candidate following inspection of its implementation: `pc_window_ios_drawable` uses SDL SysWM UIKit information; swap binds that FBO/RBO and restores separate prior read/draw/RBO bindings; final presentation and overlay use the SDL display FBO.
- Existing P1 source is unchanged by this follow-up. No P2 Retina, resolution, audio, or settings behavior is changed.
- Local MSVC C++17 host validation passes five states using actual candidate helpers/swap code: valid target, replaced drawable, unavailable WMInfo, wrong subsystem, and null window. Prior distinct bindings restore exactly. This is simulated host validation, not OpenGL execution on iPhone.
- These P2 display changes are not included in Build #10; they await a later commit/build. No runtime black screen has been observed for P2 in this session.
- Audio clarification: P2 uses its own JSystem JAS/JAI software DSP and SDL sink. The top-level PIKMIN_NATIVE_JAUDIO=OFF does not establish missing P2 audio. Hardware audio remains unverified.
- Camera clarification: P1 touch drag is consumed without requiring Free Camera; Free Camera Speed changes the controller stick, not touch sensitivity.

pointer forwarding. All are one shared cause, different from prior pellet/route casts.

the current JKR heap with only 4-byte/default or small explicit alignment.
This violates native fundamental object alignment. The existing host allocation
scope covered GXInit and present, but not the actual first pc_gfx_flush_batch
draw where Apple's driver lazily creates compiler objects. This is the leading
evidence-based explanation for the observed crash; phone retest is required.

Candidate implemented: native C++ new/new[] heap requests use at least
alignof(std::max_align_t). Preserve larger requested alignment, negative tail
allocation direction, explicit heap routing, malloc fallback, and delete routing.
Original non-PIKI_PC_PORT console policy and raw buffer allocation APIs unchanged.
Extend existing renderer host scope to 10 boundaries: batch submission, begin
frame, five overlay texture/draw functions, full-target dim, UI draw, and blur.
Driver work within these scopes allocates from malloc rather than section heaps.
No changes to P1 source, installed app, data, settings, saves, or optional content.

## Build23 phone startup failure: audio-thread allocation batch - 2026-10-20 00:46 EDT

User updated P2 to Build23 and reports the same immediate-close symptom.
Retrieved fresh existing reports read-only after explicit connection/unlock.
Reports Nectar-pikmin2-2026-10-20-003826.ips and -003841.ips BOTH match Build23
UUIDcd97b20e-54c2-396d-9786-3535a215723d. They crash after about0.25-0.29seconds,
EXC_BAD_ACCESS/SIGBUS/EXC_ARM_DA_ALIGN on an AudioQueue thread (thread21), not
the previous main-thread Metal stack. Stack: AudioToolbox caulk concurrent
allocator -> tracking_allocator -> AQ::API::V2Impl::AllocateBuffer -> SDL
audioqueue_thread -> SDL_RunThread -> pthread. Faults are inside the480MiB JKR
arena; addresses are8-byte aligned but not16-byte aligned. No new phone files,
apps or settings were modified during diagnostic reads. P1 untouched.

Binary evidence: parsed actual Build22/23 Mach-O symbol/load-command data and
decoded MOVZ immediate arguments in ordinary new and new[]. Build22 passes4
in w2 to JKR do_alloc; Build23 passes8, not16. Thus the previous max_align_t
change under-corrected on iPhone ARM64. The Intel-host tests passed but did not
compile the fixture for the actual target. This was a Codex validation gap.
Clang's Apple max_align_t typedef is long double; Apple's ARM64 long double is
the same type format as double. Primary references:
https://clang.llvm.org/doxygen/____stddef__max__align__t_8h_source.html
https://developer.apple.com/documentation/xcode/writing-arm64-code-for-apple-platforms
The observed8-byte machine-code argument, not a host-type assumption, establishes
the actual Build23 behavior. Old graphics scopes did not transfer to SDL's
independently created AudioQueue thread. Its default global-new routing still
used the process-wide current JKR heap, risking native object lifetime/races too.

Related batch implemented:
- Native pc_object_alignment is constexpr and enforces max(16,max_align_t).
  Keep negative tail direction, larger explicit heap alignment, deletion routing,
  and original console behavior. Raw game buffer APIs unchanged.
- A thread-local game-allocation opt-in defaults FALSE on unknown native workers.
  JKRHeap::becomeCurrentHeap enables it on a game thread; pc_thread_entry scopes
  it around the actual emulated OS game callback, restoring on return/unwind.
  Native Apple/SDL threads therefore use malloc instead of the global game heap.
  This does not clear or replace the global heap pointer from a native thread.
- New pc_host_alloc.h contains noncopyable game/native host RAII scopes.
  Audio SinkLock scopes synchronous SDL/framework calls into host memory, with
  nested lock/scope restoration. beginSinkOpen and its worker explicitly use
  host allocation so the std::thread control block also avoids section heaps.
- Existing P2 graphics host scopes retained. No P1 changes or unified app.

Tests expanded from actual allocation/policy/becomeCurrentHeap/callback/scope/
audio-lock source. MSVC C++14 /W4 /WX: native1354 PASS; console1280 PASS;
Apple8-byte scalar-alignment emulation1354 PASS. Native worker default malloc,
game callback JKR opt-in, explicit heap selection, host override, exception
restoration, cross-thread isolation, audio scope nesting, and all six new forms
tested. Negative control removes16-byte floor and is rejected at compile time.
Adapter43-header audit PASS; git diff --check PASS. These are host fixtures.
CI adds Apple scalar emulation RUN plus xcrun iphoneos ARM64 C++14 syntax/static
assertion check before full game compile, catching the missed target ABI.
No local Apple SDK: actual target check/compile/link/IPA/phone retest pending.

Important source: JKRHeap.cpp; pc_host_alloc.h; dolphin_stubs/p2_os_host.cpp;
audio/jaudio_sink.cpp andjaudio_host.cpp; tests/pikmin2_native_allocator_test.py;

## 2026-10-27 — Build 78 two-phone practice feedback and follow-up

User installed Build 78 on both phones. They could connect, but practice failed: host displayed "Could not initialize practice controls", partner saw disconnected; reverse host/order also failed, another attempt showed generic "connection or loading timed out", and exiting after failure required force-closing. These are actual device results, not a successful practice test. Raye's connected iPhone was inspected read-only; no persistent co-op log was present. She was told the phone can be disconnected. No phone or save data changed.

Source investigation identified a concrete order-dependent control bug: when host session creation happens during the engine world-loaded callback, tick_end can publish and confirm the first frame before the following tick_begin configures host input, making configuration refuse. The pending fix prevents snapshot publication until configuration has completed. Added a real session test for this ordering and a code-boundary guard. Local network tests 25/25 and encrypted socket probe pass, including guest entering 10 seconds later and distinct disconnect/deadline/send errors. ## Build 79 practice findings and next batch — 2026-10-27

Physical test: host saw both captains and squads, guest view mostly black, guest inputs moved slowly/stuttered, host could rotate camera but not move after backgrounding. Both phones examined read-only; no co-op report existed. Recording reviewed; shows host practice arena and two groups stationary after return. No phone data modified.

Source findings and implemented local fixes: (1) panel latch now rearms after gameplay and on background so an interrupted session can request Ready again. (2) Input timing included only the outgoing half of round-trip delay even though the incoming world snapshot was already old. A simulation using actual input classes at 125 Hz previously rejected nearly all delayed movement at 60 ms round trip; estimating both legs passed sustained 12–100 ms round-trip simulations. Real network/hardware conditions may differ. (3) practice selection uses a challenge stage that also has a valid story ID; setup could start the landing movie, but guest world updates are held and guest movie update/camera view can therefore remain stuck. Practice now skips tutorial/landing movies, while focused actual-source fixtures preserve story and versus paths. This is a strong black-screen hypothesis, NOT a confirmed device fix. (4) added per-launch bounded, local-only practice camera/input/session diagnostics, excluding room code, device IDs and game/save content, for future failure analysis.

Local 25/25 network tests and encrypted loopback passed, actual extracted resume and movie tests passed, and Windows strict/actual game-header checks passed. ### Co-op interruption audit (2026-10-28)
- P1 room and practice lifecycle observers now track the active game window's UIScene notifications, scoped to that scene. UIKit's scene lifecycle differs from application-level lifecycle callbacks. This is a preventive correction, not a proven explanation for Build 79's freeze.
- Practice continues to pause on interruption and requires the existing readiness flow; room code does not intentionally close the link. iOS may suspend networking, so live reconnection remains unimplemented and unverified.
- Local source contract checks cover observer scoping; iOS compilation and real-device interruption/reconnect behavior remain unverified. Build 81 predates this change.
- Apple references: https://developer.apple.com/documentation/uikit/uiscene/willdeactivatenotification and https://developer.apple.com/documentation/uikit/uiscene/didactivatenotification

### Practice stale-input guard (2026-10-28, after Build 82 submission)
Practice guest input previously retained unsent taps, one-shot actions and camera drag indefinitely while publication was stalled. Source now expires transient taps/actions/drag after 300 ms; live stick/held state continues from the newest sample. Individual button timestamps preserve a newer different action as an older action expires. This is an explicit compromise: a tap during an extended stall may be dropped rather than unexpectedly fire later. Focused practice input and session tests passed (including expiry and recovery); real-header local clang analysis passed with existing Windows fopen deprecation warning. Not in Build 82, and no iOS or two-phone test yet. This does not fix all lag or guarantee safe live rejoin.


### Shared Onion validation audit (2026-10-28)
The isolated OnionAuthority foundation already tests shared stock, separate per-player limits, replay/idempotency, invalid ownership, overflow and 10,000 conservation cycles. It now also rejects scene epoch zero, consistent with active co-op sessions. Local Onion authority suite passed. This is not connected to campaign gameplay, not in Build 82, and not hardware-verified; engine-side allocation/commit and full world/save integration remain required.


### Invite privacy while switching apps (2026-10-28, after Build 82)
The P1 room screen now hides the visible private invite text when its game scene deactivates and restores it on activation without clearing the room. This reduces app-switcher preview exposure; it is not a guarantee about other apps, the share sheet, or iOS behavior. Local presentation contract passed. No iOS compilation/device test yet; change is not in Build 82. Apple discusses background scene snapshots at https://developer.apple.com/documentation/uikit/uiscene/didenterbackgroundnotification .


### Weak connection simulator audit (2026-10-28, after Build 82)
Prior in-memory reliable messages were always idealized as lossless without retransmission delay, despite simulated loss configured for unreliable messages. Added an opt-in, deterministic approximation where a selected reliable message has extra delay and later ordered messages wait behind it. This is a test model, not an exact GNS retransmission or network performance claim. Tests pass for ordered blocking and for a delayed host pause: old guest movement cannot advance the host or leak into its resumed world. Local tests only; no iPhone or WAN evidence. New test and simulator are not in Build 82.


Additional weak-connection test: under an artificial four-second one-way reliable delay, the local test session times out, freezes host authority, refuses guest commands, and does not silently revive merely because delivery resumes. This is a simulated result, not proof of device behavior or live rejoin. The simulator also resets old ordering deadlines when a new connection replaces a disconnected one. Tests added after Build 82.

Room join messages now distinguish known game, region, build, game-content and capability mismatches, and report failure to initialize the network separately. Unknown mismatches remain generic. The existing compatibility gate and independent personal settings are unchanged. Source contract check passed; native iOS and device UI validation still required. Not included in Build 82.

### Isolated ICE compile experiment (2026-10-28)
Pinned GameNetworkingSockets d534c19aa760df3fb75fd20db13ba1932b8a5463 has native ICE source and iOS platform configuration. An isolated Windows static-library build with `ENABLE_ICE=ON`, `USE_STEAMWEBRTC=OFF` and existing pinned protobuf succeeded (44 build steps; `work/local-net-win/ice-probe-build`). The first restricted Windows compiler probe failed C1902 before compiling source; a retry with normal toolchain access passed. This is Windows compile-only: no P2P session, iOS build, signaling, STUN/TURN service, relay, or internet/device test. The production iOS dependencies remain ICE=OFF, and the current transport uses `CreateListenSocketIP` / `ConnectByIPAddress`; enabling the flag alone would not switch it to the P2P signaling API. No existing app, dependencies or saves were modified. Valve guidance: https://github.com/ValveSoftware/GameNetworkingSockets/blob/master/README_P2P.md


### Shared-Onion aggregate capacity guard (after Build 83)
`OnionAuthority` now requires an explicit aggregate field limit in addition to separate player allowances. Host-side accounting rejects a withdrawal atomically when the combined field count would exceed that limit, retains shared stock, and caches the rejected result safely for retries. Tests cover independent allowances, a smaller combined limit, retry safety, explicit invalid limit and existing 10,000-cycle conservation; local suite passes. Choosing an actual P1 limit must account for engine and snapshot bounds and device performance. This isolated foundation is not connected to campaign gameplay, is not in Build 83, and has no iOS or hardware validation.

### Stale continuous-input guard (after Build 84 submission)
P1 practice input will not publish a cached held control or stick value if local sampling has stopped or its timestamp is stale for more than 300 ms. When fresh samples resume, normal held controls publish again; host-side input expiry remains responsible for releasing missing movement. Focused input/session tests and real-header local analysis passed. This is a source-level safeguard, not proof that any observed device freeze was caused by stale sampling. Not in Build 84; iOS and phone testing pending.

Shared-Onion foundation follow-up: locally tested competing same-tick requests in both host-processing orders; only available stock is granted and no duplicate is created. Campaign engine integration and real-phone behavior remain untested. Test added after Build 84 submission.


### Upstream comparison (2026-10-28)
Fetched upstream/main 77a5aaa4, with releases through 1.1. Upstream changes include P1 seed landing, P1/P2 card atomic-write work, P2 local co-op cinematic fixes, P2 missing-audio fix, and desktop/Android menu and launcher changes. Do not wholesale merge: our iOS renderer, touch, save isolation and networking differ. P2 audio was already user-reported working on our build; upstream P2 changes require separate comparison and testing.

P1 source has the steep-wall seed push already, but snapped seed landing at 30 FPS as well as 60/120. Adapted upstream commits 33b5abc7 and aa18d8a4: retain the existing steep-wall push, preserve original 30 FPS bounce, and keep higher-FPS ground snap. Upstream's small decision tests passed locally and the actual itemAI translation unit passed local real-header Clang analysis with existing Windows deprecation warnings. Workflow now runs the test in future P1 iOS builds. The change is not in Build 84 and has no iOS build or device validation yet.

Upstream atomic card writes are not yet imported: our current P1 card layer includes separate practice-save isolation and concurrent I/O guards. A safe adaptation must test old-save preservation, metadata consistency, short/full disk writes, rename failures, delayed worker jobs and interrupted save stages on isolated fixtures before any integration. No claim that a simple file flush guarantees power-loss durability.


### Isolated P1 interrupted-save recovery test (after Build 84)
Added `tests/pikmin1_card_recovery_test.py`, which extracts the actual `DoSave` and `DoList` functions and links the actual P1 CARD backend to isolated temporary fixtures. It injects failures before create, write, status, old-file delete, and final rename, then invokes real listing recovery and checks that the complete expected old or new bytes remain accessible. Local Windows fixture passed for these controlled stages; no actual phone files were opened or changed. This does NOT simulate failure partway through a write, filesystem rename, metadata pair, OS termination or power loss; it does not prove durable/atomic saving. The new test is included in the next P1 native-build check, not Build 84. Upstream atomic card-write adaptation remains under investigation and was not applied.
